- **Spoken · Yagami Takuya** (p.103) — “Aside from Ayanokōji-senpai, have you made peace with Horikita-senpai?” — Yagami.
- **Spoken** (p.104) — “I won't answer that either.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Yagami Takuya** (p.104) — “By the looks of it, you've been heartbroken. I'm a little disappointed, Kushida-senpai.” — Yagami.
- **Spoken** (p.104) — “I am…!” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Yagami Takuya** (p.104) — “Oh, that's okay. You don't have to say anything else. I can tell by looking at you.” — Yagami; his dismissive attitude lacked some of his former politeness.
- **Caveat** (p.104) — “Rather, she was clearly more tolerant than the average student, perhaps due to her repeated contact with unusual people like Ayanokōji, Ryūen, or Amasawa.” — the narration; she was surprised and felt a sense of realization when acting tough.
- **Spoken** (p.104) — “This is the end of us, Yagami-kun. We have nothing to do with each other, right?” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Yagami Takuya** (p.104) — “Rest assured. You're worried that I might go around exposing Kushida-senpai's past, right? That's why you came to check on me while giving me a warning, right?” — Yagami.
- **Spoken** (p.104) — “That's right, if Yagami-kun exposes me, rumors about me will go around the school.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Yagami Takuya** (p.104) — “Then will you listen to what I have to say?” — Yagami.
- **Spoken** (p.104) — “I'll tell them everything about Yagami-kun, about how you used me to get Ayanokōji and Horikita expelled from school, about how you're a devil with a kind face.” — Kushida; Yagami didn't know if this was a threat.
- **Spoken · Yagami Takuya** (p.104) — “You have threatened back. Then I will keep that in mind. Are we done?” — Yagami.
- **Spoken · Yagami Takuya** (p.104) — “I'm the leader of the 1st year Class B. I'm busy with various cultural festival stalls, so I'll see you then.” — Yagami; he broke off the conversation and walked away.
- **Spoken** (p.104) — “Don't forget, Yagami-kun, that as long as you keep your promise, I will keep mine.” — Kushida.
- **Caveat** (p.104) — “I hope this is the end of it.” — Kushida's wistful thinking; she also realized that it wasn't the end.
- **Narration** (p.105) — “No. I can't stop Yagami...” — Kushida.
- **Caveat** (p.105) — “Up until now, Kushida had challenged and lost to various opponents, including Horikita.” — the narration.
- **Narration** (p.105) — “I realize that I am alone. But even so, the situation has changed drastically. The other side is definitely licking Kushida's chops. Not just on the surface, but from the bottom of their hearts.” — Kushida's inner monologue.
- **Caveat** (p.105) — "Even so, she prided herself on being able to read such things." — the narration.
- **Narration** (p.105) — “Before I fight that guy, I have something to do.” — Kushida.
- **Caveat** (p.105) — "She knew that the problem that needed to be solved goes above just Yagami. She had no desire to go back to being a gentle honor student, but she must show a solid contribution to maintain a firm position in the class. Kushida Kikyo knew how to survive by herself." — the narration.
- **Relationship · Yagami Takuya** (p.101–104) — Yagami — the desperate interrogator; the unanimous-exam night call; the lone wolf from the 1st year Class B; the warning and counter-warning.

#### p.121–130 — The cookie run; the hidden meeting; the 3 p.m. warning

§5 — Ayanokōji lets her out of customer service for a while — she has an idea; the waiting customers are just bored, and they're probably hungry too; the only solution was to make the visitors wait in line; the cookie run begins. Mii-chan came out of the room, looking busy; Kushida-san, who was supposed to be organizing the line, had disappeared; Yōsuke called out to the guests in line. The indoor pool facility — Kushida was dressed in an out-of-place maid's outfit; Amasawa was holding her.

- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.121) — “What are you going to do?” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.121–122) — “The waiting customers are just bored, and they're showing a strong interest in the maid café. But they're probably hungry, too, and it's too much to ask them to leave.” — Kushida.
- **Caveat** (p.122) — Since it was also right around lunchtime, it was obvious from the adults now in the classroom that many of them were there for food and drinks; Kushida picked up one of the bags filled with homemade cookies that she has been selling, prepared for souvenirs and started walking down the hallway with it.
- **Spoken** (p.122) — “Sorry to keep you waiting” — Kushida; she then pulled a cookie out of the bag and started handing them out to the waiting crowd.
- **Caveat** (p.124) — If Kushida were to leave her current post, it would not be difficult for someone to run away from the line with some guilt on their back, but she stayed and continued to talk to them with a smile on her face; after receiving the cookies, it was no longer easy to leave the line, even though they were impatient.
- **Caveat** (p.124) — She could see what was going on in the restaurant better than anyone else; what could she do to get as many people on her side as possible?
- **Caveat** (p.124) — She knew how to get close to adults of the opposite sex, engaged them in conversation that made them feel good, and sometimes even held their hand or did other skinship with them; the other girls had been working hard all day, but Kushida was the only one who had managed to do all of these things perfectly; even when she was accounting at times, she made as few mistakes as possible.
- **Spoken · Hirata Yōsuke** (p.124) — “Kushida-san's abilities are quite something. This is her element, I guess.” — Yōsuke.
- **Spoken · Hirata Yōsuke** (p.124) — “It seems that Kushida-san and Horikita-san, who have been facing strong headwinds, will also have some tailwinds.” — Yōsuke.
- **Spoken · Hirata Yōsuke** (p.125) — “Excuse me, have you seen a girl standing in line here?” — Yōsuke called out to the guests in line.
- **Caveat** (p.125) — “Oh, you mean the girl who was handing out cookies? It looks like she was approached by a girl from the same school and followed her about five minutes ago.” — a guest; “What was she like?” — “Um, a girl with her hair tied up in two knots.”
- **Spoken** (p.125) — “So don't make me say it again…” — Kushida, shouting at Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.126) — “Wow…” — meanwhile, the other person immediately noticed him and told Kushida to stop talking.
- **Spoken** (p.126) — “What? Why is... Ayanokōji-kun here..?” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.126) — “ Of course he's going to look for the ace when you've disappeared.” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.126) — “I thought I had taken her to a secretive location, but I'm surprised you found this place, senpai.” — Amasawa.
- **Caveat** (p.126) — He was actually keeping an eye on her the moment she stepped out into the line.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.126) — “Unfortunately for you, I've created an alliance with someone I can rely on now. No matter where anyone goes, I'll be sure to know where they are.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.126) — “She was going to go right back after this, right senpai~?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken** (p.126) — “Yeah. She's right. I'm sorry I slipped out without telling you, but I also wanted to talk to Amasawa-san for a minute.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.126) — “Then you could have just stood there and talked, that's no reason to leave for 10 or 20 minutes.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.126) — “Why was Kushida so adamant about expelling me from the school in a unanimous special exam, and why did she take a reckless gamble?” — his private question; Amasawa probes his knowledge.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.126) — “You are not the least bit surprised to see me and Kushida-senpai together, are you? Did you know about our history?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.126) — “No.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.126) — “But today, with this timely contact, I understand everything. Even information that seemed unnecessary was derived in my head on its own.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.127) — “Kushida-senpai will pay you back later, so can you give me a little time?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken** (p.127) — “Sorry Ayanokōji-kun, could you please excuse me. I'll be back as soon as I can. I also really need to talk to Amasawa-san.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.127) — “I see what you mean, but it's not going to happen. This is enough, Amasawa.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.127) — “Senpai's eyes are so naughty, aren't they? You're looking at me like I'm naked or something~” — Amasawa; she pressed the tip of her finger against her lips in a seductive manner, but the tone wasn't sexual.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.127) — “Kushida, you have a weakness regarding your past with Amasawa and one other person. That's why you forced the class to get into an uproar so that Horikita and I would be expelled from the unanimous special exam. Or maybe they were working on something before that.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.127) — “Eh?” — Kushida simply looked surprised.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.127) — “Let's stop now, senpai. This is a time for me and Kushida-senpai.” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.127) — “I'm sorry, but it doesn't work that way. Kushida is a necessary part of the class, even before her work as a maid.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.127) — “What do you mean by that?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.127–128) — “You may be right, but I'm not so sure about the other one.” — as she replied, Amasawa's demeanour changed for the first time; without warning, Amasawa, with an eerie smile on her face, grabbed Kushida's wrist tightly.
- **Spoken** (p.127) — “What?!” — Kushida.
- **Caveat** (p.127–128) — Amasawa pulled her close and stood behind Kushida with her right hand locked in place and forcefully closed Kushida's mouth with her left hand.
- **Spoken** (p.128) — “Nngh!” — Kushida; her pain.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “Perhaps you have an idea who the other student is, senpai?” — Amasawa.
- **Caveat** (p.128) — Kushida's words were silenced before she could ask the question since Kushida knew that person firsthand.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “You know, Kushida-senpai, if you say anything bad, I'll have you expelled, okay?” — Amasawa; Kushida's face contorted in pain.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.128) — “This isn't like you, Amasawa. You seem like you've been cornered pretty hard.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “Wait, senpai, I didn't say anything, did I?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.128) — “Every single action speaks for itself.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “Let's talk about it again later, just the two of us next time. Please pretend you didn't see this and leave, Ayanokōji-senpai. If you do that, I'll let her go in about ten minutes.” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.128) — “What if I don't say yes?” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “If you don't say yes, I might cripple Kushida-senpai here.” — Amasawa; she said and squeezed her right arm even tighter.
- **Spoken** (p.128) — “Nngh!” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “I'm a pretty girl, but I can easily break an arm or two.” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.128) — “Then let's give it a try. Let's see if you break Kushida's arm first, or if I can stop you.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.128) — The distance between them was about 5 meters.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “Are you serious?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.128) — “Are you serious about breaking her arm? Or are you saying that you don't think I can stop you?” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.128) — “Both.” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.128) — “Then you're wrong about both. You really shouldn't forget who I am.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.128–129) — Laughing, Amasawa loosened her grip on Kushida's right hand, even if only slightly; at that moment he kicked the ground and dove in just as Amasawa switched to a motion to break her arm; his right hand slid down Kushida's arm and reached her wrist as his left hand went around her mouth to her back, he grabbed Amasawa's right hand.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.129) — “No way...” — Amasawa; it must have been a defensive instinct.
- **Caveat** (p.129) — In an instant, she abandoned the action of breaking Kushida's arm and shifted her attention to him, and tried to make a left fist with a tight grip.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.129) — “Fuu~!” — the forceful hold on the ground caused Amasawa to lose her breath for a moment and gasp for air; her breath caused a slight dust cloud to rise.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.129) — “Oh my, that was... a little unexpected.” — Amasawa; Amasawa's pride, which was always high, was deeply wounded.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.129) — “Did you think there wasn't much difference between you and me?” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.129) — “You mean I was wrong about your abilities?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.129) — “Probably.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.129) — “I'll tell you this, you seem to think that the other student might push me out of school, but did you ever wonder why I never asked for the other student's name?” — Ayanokōji; the smile slowly faded from Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.129) — “You're serious, aren't you, senpai?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.129) — “It's not you who doesn't understand that, right, Amasawa?” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.129) — Even with only half-hearted martial-arts practice, he would not have had any real feeling for it yet; but Amasawa was different; in less than 10 seconds of total movement, the match had already been decided by a wide margin.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.129–130) — “You and the other student should have challenged me at an early stage. You shouldn't have gone around and involved the people around you in the fun.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.130) — “So, you understood why I contacted Kushida-senpai...” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “It all connected just now. And now the unexpected is about to happen.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.130) — “The unexpected?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “After 3 p.m., keep an eye on the student council chambers. You are not to be seen in front of anyone. Then you'll have all the answers.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “We've wasted a lot of time. Let's go back to the maid cafe.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Amasawa Ichika** (p.130) — “Is it okay leaving her?” — Amasawa.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “It's okay. You don't have to worry about your past being exposed.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.130) — “How could Ayanokōji-kun know that?” — Kushida rushed after him.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “Don't worry about it, but you can trust me.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.130) — “Who is Ayanokōji-kun?” — Kushida.
- **Spoken** (p.130) — “I don't know anything about fighting, but I can tell you you're not normal.” — Kushida.
- **Caveat** (p.130) — That question would be inevitable if one had witnessed the conversation and fight with Amasawa earlier.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “It's not uncommon for classmates to learn martial arts. Horikita and Ibuki, even Ryūen and Akito should be strong in fights, even if they are self-taught. It's not like boys and girls can compete with each other from the start.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.130) — “Yeah, sure.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken** (p.130) — “Thank you for helping me.” — Kushida.
- **Caveat** (p.130) — An unexpected thanks from Kushida.
- **Spoken** (p.130) — “You don't think I'm sincerely grateful, but that's fine. I just felt like saying so, even if it's a lie.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “It's not a big deal. It's more of a natural behaviour for a classmate.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.130) — “Then you don't have to consider this as a debt, right?” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.130) — “Of course.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.130) — If he did consider her to be in his debt because of what happened, she wouldn't really be able to repay him.
- **Relationship · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.121–130) — The cookie run; the pool grab; the wrist lock; the 3 p.m. warning.
- **Relationship · Amasawa Ichika** (p.125–130) — The indoor pool grab; the wrist lock; the White Room score comparison.

#### p.131–135 — The staffing crisis; the 600-point warning

§1 — the maid café overcrowded; Kushida recovered brilliantly and held the line; the overcrowding caused a shortage of staff; the maids, who had taken a one-hour break, were still tired; their movements had slowed considerably.

- **Caveat** (p.131) — Sato and Mii-chan were the aces of the team; Horikita, who was not initially supposed to be in charge of the hall, started serving customers in the middle of the day; the remaining three were Ishikura, who had substituted for Matsushita, Kushida, as well as Inokashira, who specialized in handing out flyers.
- **Spoken** (p.131) — “Ayanokōji-kun, the waiting customers may have grown bored. I don't think we can keep them tied up like this forever.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Horikita Suzune** (p.131) — “What's going on at the end of the line?” — Horikita.
- **Spoken** (p.131) — “We told them that they would have to wait for a long time, and while some of them will wait, most of them will leave.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken** (p.132) — “You had two extra maid outfits, didn't you?” — Kushida.
- **Spoken** (p.132) — “Yeah, why not Karuizawa-san?” — Kushida; she thought Kei, his girlfriend, would listen to his instructions.
- **Caveat** (p.132) — “As I recall, she has a break at 2 p.m., right?” — “Yes. She's on break right now, and even if we had her change her clothes after returning at 3:00, it's doubtful to what extent she'd be able to be of any use.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken · Ike Kanji** (p.132) — “Oh no, I heard you say you're short on staff right now. ……. I was wondering if you could leave it to Satsuki.” — Ike; Shinohara-san? he wonders if she's up to the task.
- **Spoken · Ike Kanji** (p.132) — “I think she'll be fine. Besides, she was practising to be a maid, even if only lightly.” — Ike.
- **Caveat** (p.132) — Later, with a recommendation from Shinohara, he strongly persuaded Azuma; It was decided that she would join us.
- **Spoken · Horikita Suzune** (p.132) — “Ayanokōji-kun, as you know, I have to take a break at 3:00. I'm going to need some manpower after I leave.” — Horikita.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.132) — “I've thought it through, don't worry.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.132–133) — Fifteen minutes later, Shinohara was asked to go to the hall, and Azuma was asked to join Kushida in the hallway to hold up the customers waiting there; but Kushida's expression in the hallway was grim, and it was not a development she seemed to relish.
- **Spoken** (p.133) — “It's hard to say if she's the right person for the job, because Shinohara-san …  doesn't make …  much of an impact visually, and she's not very good as customer service.” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.133) — “There's an emergency.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.133) — “Hasebe-san is still unavailable?” — Kushida.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.133) — “Before I say 'not available,' she's been gone since this morning. She's formally participating in the festival, but she might be back at her dormitory.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.133) — “You mean payback for Sakura-san's expulsion? You participated in the preliminary discussion, right?”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.133) — “I was just observing.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.133) — “Still, that means you know more than Shinohara-san, Azuma-san and the others, right?”
- **Caveat** (p.133) — “That's why it was an effective way to get back at Haruka and Akito who seemed to be along for the ride, since we were making our plans based on the calculations of our strength.” — Ayanokōji; “I see. If you knew that much, I would have thought that you would have considered the possibility that those two would not participate and thought of another way.” — “Even if you knew that, you can't increase the class size. Besides, if we moved with another strategy from the beginning, Haruka and Akito would've got the hint. We decided that it would be more of a disadvantage to have them go on an unexpected sabotage by doing so.” — “It would bother us, but that's it. It's not an action that can be called revenge.” — “If only this were the case.” — “What do you mean?” — “Haruka and Airi were looking forward to the cultural festival. That's why they were going to see it through until the end. Since that's over, there will be no reason to continue staying at this school.”
- **Spoken** (p.133–134) — “You mean they're going to drop out of school?”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.134) — “Probably, if two students voluntarily withdraw from the school, in addition to the simple disadvantage in numbers, a significant drop in class points will be inevitable. The class would be severely damaged.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.134) — “How much damage?”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.134) — “I estimate 600 class points for the two of them combined.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.134) — “Six hundred?”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.134) — “Nothing surprising. Expulsion under the normal rules of this school has traditionally been penalized by that much.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Caveat** (p.135) — Excluding limited circumstances where the risk of expulsion was high due to strict special exams, this was a natural step.
- **Spoken** (p.134) — “If two people really drop out, it means that my path to Class A is doomed.”
- **Spoken** (p.134) — “It's going to be almost impossible to get back in.”
- **Spoken** (p.134) — “I wonder if they're just going to sit back and watch.”
- **Caveat** (p.135) — “I was going to come up with a way out of this.” — “I guess there was some unforeseen trouble, or maybe the trump card never arrived.” — Ayanokōji; Haruka's strategy of sabotaging the festival, or rather voluntarily leaving school, was basically like an unstoppable ultimatum.
- **Caveat** (p.135) — “But Haruka did not take an unreasonable strategy. She knew that her skills were not as good as mine, so she chose the most efficient strategy.” — the narration.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.135) — “Are you sure you want to continue like this?” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.135) — “That's not for me to decide; that's for Haruka and Akito to decide. If they want to continue with their non-participation in the festival, then that's also what they're going to have to do.”
- **Spoken** (p.135) — “I don't think …  Ayanokōji-kun …  really thinks that way, though.”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.135) — “Do you understand?” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.135) — “Do I understand? You're not going to abandon Hasebe-san and the others, are you?”
- **Caveat** (p.135) — “Apparently, Kushida could see what I was about to do.” — the narration; the “I” is Ayanokōji's reference to himself, rendered with Kushida as the subject in the narration.
- **Spoken** (p.135) — “The reason you didn't try to persuade her until this time was to test the two of them?”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.135) — “I didn't know what they were aiming for. Were they going to ruin the festival or not? But from the fact that they've done nothing so far, I had a pretty good guess. I'm going to make contact now.” — Ayanokōji.
- **Spoken** (p.135) — “Do you have any idea where they are?”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.135) — “That's why I'm having my contact figure things out” — Ayanokōji; he showed her his cell phone screen and showed her someone's message with Haruka's current location.
- **Spoken** (p.135) — “You have a reliable ally. I guess it's thanks to this person that you found out …  where I was.”
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.135) — “Ah. They're the perfect person to look or keep an eye on someone.” — Ayanokōji; they always knew where Haruka and the others were.
- **Spoken · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.135) — “But at the end of the day, there's only so much I can do. Whether or not I can make those two people's hearts beat is another matter altogether. I'm leaving.” — Ayanokōji.

#### p.145–151 — The Keyaki Mall walk-in with the SNS; her regret

§1 continues — dressed as a maid, which was out of place in this tense atmosphere, she slowly approached. §2 — Kushida was walking around handing out new cookies while serving customers with a smile; both the old and young were all gazing at Kushida; they seemed to be enjoying her company; I feel bad for Azuma, who was working hard together with Kushida.

#### p.46–62 — Boarding; the Class A challenge; the lift talk

Her name lands on the group-6 list; she's in Ayanokōji's group with Ryūen, Kitō, Nishino and the others — an uncomfortable pairing by every public measure. At the airport she makes the opening moves: rejoices with Amikura, greets Nishino politely, cool-eyes Ryūen, and organizes the boys first ("I think it's better to seat us by gender."). Across the aisle: the trip joins Haneda–Hokkaido; the etiquette-bag banter happens without her; she sits with the girls. She warns him against the advanced slope (70% hard humps), then takes the lift with him because someone must; she spells out her read-people theory and its one flaw. She pries him about expulsion; he gives her nothing (he plays along with the ambiguity); she concedes she can't read him; he gifts her two truths (Yagami & Amasawa); the lift opens; her hat escapes; his hand catches it; their fingers almost touch; she thanks him stiffly.

#### p.70–89 — The bath scene (from the other POV); girls' visit; the pillow fight

The outdoor bath: she poses as Rokkaku's friend for show; when Rokkaku leaves she drops the mask; needles Horikita about her wide friend circle and her "lucky" Ayanokōji seat; Ibuki crash-lands and demands a race; she plays referee. She presses Ichinose about her love life with fake innocence (she knows exactly whom Ichinose likes); Ichinose dodges; she praises Ichinose's circle-wide presence; the endurance bath ends when Ichinose, called back inside, returns to order them up. She leads four girls to the boys' room (“Here we are~”); apologizes for late soaking; she agrees to Ichinose's gathering with polite enthusiasm.

#### p.130–139 — Sapporo: the spot-tour debate and the strategy meeting

The bus stops at Sapporo Station; each group gets a pamphlet of 15 spots; the deal: visit six, photograph each; 20+ points = 30,000 private points; fewer than six spots and the group loses tomorrow's free day (study session instead); groups must travel as a unit. The eight of them split: Ryūen wants the points; Kitō wants nothing to do with it; Nishino and Amikura want fun; she bridges both camps; Ayanokōji proposes the majority-vote solution; she covers for his strategy session from Watanabe's jealous sideways glance.

- **Caveat** (p.170) — Her position in the class wasn’t illustrious, so visibly contributing wasn’t a bad thing; the narration treats her as a genuinely reassuring presence.
- **Spoken · Kushida Kikyō** (p.171) — “What about trying to protect her once more? I think she might be aiming for it.”
- **Caveat** (p.171) — The answer back to her — “Do you think so from the flow so far?” — is printed without a name; none assigned.
- **Spoken · Kushida Kikyō** (p.171) — “Not really. I just judged it based on what I’ve seen of Ichinose-san up until now.”
- **Relationship · Horikita Suzune** (p.168–171) — Her supply line for target intelligence; Horikita wields her without auditing the source — the trust is total and it works.
- **Caveat** (p.170) — Makida’s admiration (he clapped) belongs to her read on Satonaka, not to anyone else in the scene.

### Year 3, Volume 1

#### p.25–28 — Kushida: Ayanokōji was more important than assumed

- **Spoken** (p.25) — "That's a possibility."
- **Spoken** (p.25) — "Ike-kun, Shinohara-san, sorry, but Ayanokouji-kun is much more important than you two think."
- **Spoken** (p.25) — "Just because it hasn't come to light doesn't mean he hasn't contributed to the class in countless unseen ways."
- **Spoken** (p.27) — "You seem quite calm, Hirata-kun. Whether it's a transfer or expulsion, a student may have disappeared from our class, and yet you're not disturbed."
- **Trait** (p.25–28) — Calm amidst the chaos and aware of Ayanokōji's hidden contributions; pointedly questions Hirata's uncharacteristic composure and pushes to end the homeroom discussion before eavesdroppers overhear.

- **Relationship · Hirata Yōsuke** (p.27) — Needles him for being too calm about the disappearance.

#### p.126–132 — Kushida needles the gloomy Horikita

Buys a café au lait to go, spots Horikita gloomy on a bench, and stops.

- **Spoken** (p.127) — "I'm not going to ask what you're doing here. You're waiting to bump into Ayanokouji-kun by pretending it's a coincidence, aren't you?"
- **Spoken** (p.127) — "No, it's totally obvious."
- **Spoken** (p.127) — "I'd like to leave you alone, but I can't overlook such a gloomy face."
- **Spoken** (p.131) — "Even in these circumstances, a class leader is supposed to win regardless."
- **Spoken** (p.131) — "You can wait here for Ayanokouji-kun to come back. But… I doubt he'll care."
- **Trait** (p.126–132) — Cynical but pragmatic — her true nature exposed, increasingly isolated and short-tempered; needling Horikita, she still wants her class leader to win (needed for her own Class A graduation), and delivers cold parting advice.
- **Relationship · Horikita Suzune** (p.126–132) — Sees through her, criticizes her state, urges her to focus on the exam, then leaves coldly.
- **Relationship · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.127, 131) — Knows Horikita is waiting for him; doubts he cares.
- **Relationship · Ninomiya Yui** (p.130) — Politely says goodbye to the Class 3-D student, smiling until she's out of earshot — the two-faced act.

#### p.216–223 — Kushida's dusk meeting: Ayanokōji's offer of betrayal

Waits alone leaning on the railing at dusk, having been called out by Ayanokōji; refuses to turn around at first.

- **Spoken** (p.216) — "When we first entered school, you saw quite a lot of things you shouldn't have, Ayanokouji-kun."
- **Spoken** (p.220) — "It was something like an accident, but I was really anxious when you threatened me."
- **Spoken** (p.220) — "Really? You've been thinking about setting me up since then, haven't you?"
- **Spoken** (p.221) — "I have no such intentions. Really."
- **Spoken** (p.221) — "What? Are you seriously asking me to betray my class?"
- **Spoken** (p.221) — "Because of our previous Private Point transaction, I ended up in trouble. Do you think I would cooperate with you again now that you've become an enemy?"
- **Spoken** (p.221) — "Do you think that counts as a threat? Ryuuen-kun knows about it too."
- **Spoken** (p.222) — "You don't even need my strength to take down Horikita's pathetic class."
- **Spoken** (p.222) — "Huh? You sure think highly of her, don't you?"
- **Spoken** (p.222) — "Expulsions… from our class?"
- **Spoken** (p.222) — "That's, like, really risky. Even if I get a little allowance on the side and drag down the class, it's meaningless if I can't graduate from Class A."
- **Spoken** (p.222) — "How serious are you?"
- **Spoken** (p.223) — "What's that about? So what do you want, Ayanokouji-kun? You want to bring down Class A, right?"
- **Spoken** (p.223) — "I don't really get it, but it seems like you just want things to go your way."
- **Trait** (p.216–223) — Guarded and two-faced — evades his questions, notes she was anxious when he threatened her two years ago, refuses the betrayal offer citing their last transaction's trouble, yet never turns to face him and masks her true intentions; her " superficial skepticism" hides genuine calculation.
- **Relationship · Ayanokōji Kiyotaka** (p.216–223) — Is recruited as an insider; rejects the offer on the surface but leaves the door open, unable to read whether he is bluffing.
- **Relationship · Horikita Suzune** (p.222) — Disparages her as "pathetic" and is surprised Ayanokōji thinks highly of her; her reunion with her same-middle-school kouhai caused her past stress.
- **Relationship · Ryūen Kakeru** (p.221) — Points out he also knows her secret, but his rumors would lack credibility.
